Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science · FORMAL_ISSUE

Integrating functional accessibility and pedestrian environmental comfort: A spatially explicit framework for urban walkability evaluation

Qi Cao; Vincent Tourre; Thomas Leduc; Myriam Servières

卷期
V53 · I9
门控
FORMAL_ASSIGNMENT
DOI
10.1177/23998083251413000
证据层级
OFFICIAL_DIRECTORY

官方论文页面journals.sagepub.com · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Integrating functional accessibility and pedestrian environmental comfort: A spatially explicit framework for urban walkability evaluation

期刊：Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science · DOI：10.1177/23998083251413000 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Urban walkability is central to sustainable urban development and, at the city scale, is increasingly viewed as shaped by both functional accessibility and pedestrian environmental comfort (PEC). However, most citywide assessments remain predominantly function-oriented, and few provide explicit behavioral validation using observed walking patterns. This study addresses this gap by proposing a spatially explicit, dual-dimensional framework that treats functional accessibility and PEC as co-equal components of walkability. Using Nantes, France, as a case study, we implement the framework on a uniform 150 m × 150 m grid and derive three composite indices—Traditional Walkability Score (TWS), a PEC index, and a PEC-enhanced Walkability Score (PEWS)—from GIS indicators standardized and combined using entropy-based weighting. Our analysis reveals a systematic spatial mismatch between functional and environmental dimensions: TWS concentrates in amenity-rich and well-connected areas, whereas PEC is highest along blue–green corridors. The integrated PEWS induces a targeted spatial re-ranking, deprioritizing functionally rich but environmentally harsh central areas while upgrading nature-rich peripheral zones. We then behaviorally validate the framework using observed pedestrian counts and gridded resident population data from INSEE, operationalized as a deviation metric comparing observed flows with population-based expectations. Blue–green features (trees, rivers, and green spaces) are associated with higher-than-expected pedestrian activity, whereas proximity to major motorized roads is associated with lower-than-expected activity. These findings support PEC as a necessary component of city-scale walkability assessment and provide actionable guidance for planning interventions.

Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science · FORMAL_ISSUE

Who owns the flood plain? A parcel-level analysis of organizational form and flood risk

Jesse R. Andrews; Jenny B. Mason; Elaine Yu; Zhenghong Tang

卷期
V53 · I9
门控
FORMAL_ASSIGNMENT
DOI
10.1177/23998083251414554
证据层级
OFFICIAL_DIRECTORY

官方论文页面journals.sagepub.com · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Who owns the flood plain? A parcel-level analysis of organizational form and flood risk

期刊：Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science · DOI：10.1177/23998083251414554 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Regulatory flood maps define the geography of hazard but obscure the organizational structure of exposure. This study examines how regulatory flood exposure is distributed across owner-of-record forms and local portfolio footprints using the complete 2022 inventory of single-family residential parcels in Douglas County, Nebraska (N = 183,313). We classify owners into five organizational forms (including Individuals, LLCs, and Corporations) and distinguish between single-parcel and multi-parcel local portfolios. While only 1.10% of parcels fall within FEMA Special Flood Hazard Areas (SFHAs) in aggregate, prevalence varies sharply by ownership structure. Unadjusted SFHA rates are highest among single-parcel Limited Liability Companies (4.83%) and single-parcel corporations (4.07%), compared to 0.81% for single-parcel individuals. In modified Poisson models with neighborhood fixed effects, LLC-owned parcels retain elevated exposure relative to individuals (adjusted Risk Ratio for single-parcel LLCs = 1.67; 95% CI: 1.31–2.13). Multi-parcel individuals and trusts also exhibit elevated prevalence. These findings demonstrate that flood exposure is not just a spatial attribute but an organizational one, concentrated within specific legal structures. Consequently, effective flood governance requires implementation strategies capable of navigating diverse legal forms and portfolio scales for mitigation and compliance.

Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science · FORMAL_ISSUE

Understanding the spatial structure and evolution of Kaifeng based on entropy and fractal analysis

Xueke Ma; Zhijun Song; Liming Liu; Xiaoqian Liu; Haiying Wang

卷期
V53 · I9
门控
FORMAL_ASSIGNMENT
DOI
10.1177/23998083261416008
证据层级
OFFICIAL_DIRECTORY

官方论文页面journals.sagepub.com · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Understanding the spatial structure and evolution of Kaifeng based on entropy and fractal analysis

期刊：Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science · DOI：10.1177/23998083261416008 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Given China’s rapid urbanization in the 21st century, understanding the complexity of its spatial structure is crucial for appropriately regulating the country’s urban development. Entropy and fractal analysis are two important quantitative means for describing the complexity of geographical space. These two methods can be integrated to comprehensively analyze the characteristics of self-organization and evolution of the urban spatial structure, as well as the internal factors driving this process. As a typical single-center city in the Central Plain of the country, Kaifeng City is representative of the rapid urbanization of China in the past four decades. This study uses data on Kaifeng City from 1980 to 2020 to examine the spatial entropy of the structure and evolution of its built-up land, and to subject it to a multi-fractal analysis. The results show that the spatial entropy of built-up land in Kaifeng City has been increasing in the last four decades, and the degree to which its urban space is filling up has been increasing each year. The spatial entropy of the urban center is higher than that of the surrounding areas, while built-up areas in the region have become more orderly. The multi-fractal analysis further reveals that Kaifeng City has undergone prominent spatial expansion, and its built-up land has expanded particularly rapidly in the past two decades. Changes in the central and marginal areas of the city have been asynchronous and periodic, where the marginal areas have undergone a disorderly expansion. The heterogeneity of urban space has gradually decreased. The work here provides a comprehensive understanding of the patterns of urban spatial expansion as well as the structure of land use.

Mapping linguistic landscapes and displacement: Urban-scale analysis of internally displaced people in Kyiv using street view imagery and computer vision

Ryan James Scamehorn; Ron Mahabir; Chanida Suwanprasit; Sakda Homhuan; Liwa Pardthaisong

卷期
V53 · I9
门控
FORMAL_ASSIGNMENT
DOI
10.1177/23998083251415461
证据层级
OFFICIAL_DIRECTORY

官方论文页面journals.sagepub.com · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Mapping linguistic landscapes and displacement: Urban-scale analysis of internally displaced people in Kyiv using street view imagery and computer vision

期刊：Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science · DOI：10.1177/23998083251415461 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Internally displaced people (IDPs) who fled conflict zones in Donetsk and Luhansk beginning in 2013 relocated to other cities in Ukraine. Their Russian-language preference can be quantitatively and qualitatively observed and measured in the urban textual environment, or linguistic landscape (LL) of Kyiv, the bilingual Ukrainian capital. This study examines the LL at a city-wide scale, leveraging Google Street View imagery with deep learning text detection, recognition, and script identification, alongside geospatial tools for large-scale analysis. The approach enables systematic, reproducible mapping of machine-detectable public-facing signage along road networks, supporting analysis of spatial and temporal associations between population relocation and LL change. Analyses at the administrative and road network levels reveal differing language preferences between primary and residential roads. Results indicate clustering of the Russian keyword ‘аренда’ (rent) near IDP relocation sites during the relocation period and an observable increase in Ukrainian-language preference after the implementation of the 2019 language policy.

Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science · FORMAL_ISSUE

Quantity or structure? Asset or burden? Causal evidence of street greenery’s impact on housing prices from sequential data

Zhaocheng Bai; Rui Ji; Feng Gao; Yuheng Mao; Song Liu

卷期
V53 · I9
门控
FORMAL_ASSIGNMENT
DOI
10.1177/23998083261420306
证据层级
OFFICIAL_DIRECTORY

官方论文页面journals.sagepub.com · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Quantity or structure? Asset or burden? Causal evidence of street greenery’s impact on housing prices from sequential data

期刊：Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science · DOI：10.1177/23998083261420306 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Street greenery, as critical green infrastructure, provides extensive ecosystem services, and accurately evaluating its value benefits urban greening strategies and funding decisions. Previous studies relying on cross-sectional data and hedonic regression models are limited by confounding factors, thus failing to accurately measure the net effects of street greenery characteristics on housing prices. To address this limitation, hedonic regression, propensity score matching and difference-in-differences methodologies were employed based on housing price and built environment data from 2017 to 2022 to analyze how street greenery quantity and vegetation structure affect housing prices. Results indicated that street greenery impacts differ significantly between the central area and the expanded central area, revealing a negative price effect defined as the “Green Burden Effect” in expanded central area. Additionally, greenery quantity changes exerted stronger and more stable price impacts compared to vegetation structure. The DID analysis further clarified temporal changes in these relationships. Employing multiple econometric models revealed the complex spatial-temporal relationships between street greenery and housing prices, emphasizing the importance of spatially targeted greening strategies to optimize residential values and promote equitable urban development.

Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science · FORMAL_ISSUE

Mapping urban vitality through dynamic population presence: A social media-based analysis in Tallinn

Kofoworola Modupe Osunkoya; Tuomas Väisänen; Jenni Partanen; Olle Järv

卷期
V53 · I9
门控
FORMAL_ASSIGNMENT
DOI
10.1177/23998083261420323
证据层级
OFFICIAL_DIRECTORY

官方论文页面journals.sagepub.com · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Mapping urban vitality through dynamic population presence: A social media-based analysis in Tallinn

期刊：Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science · DOI：10.1177/23998083261420323 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Urban vitality (UV) is a commonly used concept that describes the liveliness, diversity, and attractiveness of urban spaces, which foster social interaction and activity. While the concept has received ample attention from researchers, combining big data and traditional data sources to capture UV remains underexplored. To address this gap, we build on contemporary interpretations of Jane Jacobs’s concept of vitality to develop a methodology that identifies dynamically vibrant areas. We propose a conceptual framework comprising three components: the built environment, the static residential population, and the dynamic population presence. The dynamic population presence, derived from georeferenced social media activity, serves as the main proxy for UV, reflecting the dynamic use and interaction within urban space. The built environment and static residential population dimensions are incorporated to contextualize and interpret the spatial patterns of dynamic presence. Vitality is further evaluated through four dynamic attributes: intensity, variability, consistency, and linguistic diversity that describe temporal rhythms and social heterogeneity. Applying this framework to Tallinn, Estonia, we explore the city’s spatio-temporal patterns and spatial clustering to identify its spatial hierarchy of vibrancy and relation to land-use functionality, mixed-use areas, and demographic diversity. The findings demonstrate how combining dynamic data with traditional indicators advances the understanding of UV and supports more inclusive, accessible, and socially cohesive urban planning.

Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science · FORMAL_ISSUE

Infrastructure, land use, and the uneven performance of public transport services

Raul Pezoa; Franco Basso; Marco Batarce; Camila Núñez; Mauricio Varas

卷期
V53 · I9
门控
FORMAL_ASSIGNMENT
DOI
10.1177/23998083261417768
证据层级
OFFICIAL_DIRECTORY

官方论文页面journals.sagepub.com · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Infrastructure, land use, and the uneven performance of public transport services

期刊：Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science · DOI：10.1177/23998083261417768 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Ensuring high-quality public transport is a key challenge for cities aiming to reduce car dependency and promote more sustainable and equitable urban mobility. This study examines how infrastructure and urban configuration shape the operational performance of public transport services, using Santiago, Chile, as a case study. We analyze three quality indicators—average speed, frequency compliance, and regularity compliance—and show that measured performance is not only a function of operator efficiency but also of structural factors beyond their control. Our findings demonstrate that external factors play an important role in quality indicators, with implications for contract design: when contracts involve relative performance comparisons between operators, contextual factors should be incorporated to avoid unfair penalization, while in systems like Santiago that use absolute standards, these factors could inform bidding decisions and performance evaluation frameworks. More broadly, our results suggest urban transport policy should consider contextual factors appropriately in contract design and performance evaluation.

Revealing implicit associations between urban form and socioeconomic indices

Jinmo Rhee; Kiarash Kiany; Alberto de Salvatierra

官方论文页面journals.sagepub.com · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Revealing implicit associations between urban form and socioeconomic indices

期刊：Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science · DOI：10.1177/23998083261420450 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

This study provides empirical evidence that parcel-level socioeconomic indices can be reliably inferred from urban form using deep learning. Building on a representation that links cadastral parcels with digital surface models, we train EfficientNet variants to predict return on investment (ROI)—used here as a representative parcel-level economic indicator—directly from volumetric form. Across more than 24,000 parcels in a North American city, the models exhibit stable convergence and consistent generalization, capturing systematic variations across residential, commercial, and industrial morphologies. The learned embeddings further reveal coherent manifolds structured by building massing, footprint geometry, and block configuration, indicating that the model extracts underlying spatial principles that correlate with economic outcomes. These findings demonstrate that urban form encodes measurable signals of economic performance and establish morphological learning as a viable pathway for integrating financial, environmental, and socio-cultural indices into form-based urban analysis and design.

Michail Georgiou; Nick Bailey; Annamaria Nizi; Jose Rafael Verduzco Torres

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

The housing market value of public transport accessibility to employment in post-pandemic England

期刊：Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science · DOI：10.1177/23998083261418330 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

The rise in home/hybrid working post-pandemic across multiple countries has been linked to significant shifts in housing demand away from urban centres. This paper explores evidence for this shift through changes in the value placed on public transport services in house prices. We analyse changes across 74 travel-to-work areas in England using data from 2.6 million house sales listings combined with novel measures of public transport accessibility to employment (PTAE). Multilevel hedonic models capture changes in willingness-to-pay for PTAE at the Travel-to-Work Area level with meta-analysis models used to analyse variations between and within cities. Overall, there was a reduction in willingness-to-pay for PTAE post-pandemic but this was confined to smaller cities and those with lower housing affordability. Within cities, reductions were greater in locations better served by public transport and with greater potential for home/hybrid working though the latter was also confined to smaller cities and those with lower affordability. Results align with evidence on increasing trends of counter-urbanisation in the UK and Europe and pose challenges for future sustainable urban development policies but also highlight the variations between urban contexts.

论文题名

Intra-urban consumption flows and sociospatial disparities: An analysis based on bank card transaction data

期刊：Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science · DOI：10.1177/23998083261420309 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

This study analyses intra-urban consumption flows in Madrid through a spatial analysis using over 689 million anonymized bank card transactions recorded in 2023. By disaggregating data by age and income groups, the research uncovers significant variations in consumer mobility patterns and spatial preferences. Older adults and high-income groups exhibit more localized consumption behaviors, while middle-income groups demonstrate wider mobility across the city. The research contributes to a growing body of work leveraging Big Data to understand complex urban behaviors at a granular scale.

论文题名

Extracting and analyzing urban housing conflicts using large language models, graph databases, and GIS

期刊：Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science · DOI：10.1177/23998083261422089 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Urban housing conflicts are increasingly shaping the social and spatial dynamics of cities, yet they remain difficult to analyze systematically due to their multi-actor complexity and fragmented representation across textual and spatial data. Existing studies tend to focus on either narrative or spatial aspects, rarely capturing the structural, temporal, and geographic dimensions of these conflicts in an integrated way. This paper addresses this gap by proposing a reproducible methodological framework that combines Large Language Models (LLMs), graph databases, and Geographic Information Systems (GIS) to analyze housing conflicts in Montréal between 2001 and 2024. 论文题名

Beyond quantity: Decoding the economic performance of underground commercial facilities in two representative Chinese cities

期刊：Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science · DOI：10.1177/23998083261421828 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

While the quantity of underground commercial facilities has been extensively studied for urban development, their economic performance, a critical indicator of sustainability, remains underexplored. This deficiency risks economically unsustainable development and wasted underground space resources. To bridge this gap, this paper developed a novel evaluation framework that leveraged openly accessible social media data (Dazhong Dianping) to assess the economic performance of underground commercial facilities. Using Geodetector and Spearman rank correlation, we analyzed multi-source numerical and categorical spatial data in Shanghai and Zhengzhou to reveal key determinants affecting the economic performance of these facilities. Transportation accessibility and commercial vibrancy were also found to be significant in shaping the economic success of underground commercial facilities. These findings offer direct guidance for evidence-based master planning and the sustainable use of urban underground space.

论文题名

Large-scale 3D building model datasets constructed from airborne LiDAR point clouds in Glasgow, UK

期刊：Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science · DOI：10.1177/23998083261452342 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Three-dimensional (3D) building models offer visual representation, interaction, analysis, and exploration of urban environment analysis. However, most cities in the United Kingdom (UK) do not have open building 3D model datasets. This study used large-scale high-density airborne LiDAR point clouds to produce 3D building models for Glasgow City. We proposed an open-source and efficient data analysis workflow that integrated a weakly supervised deep learning point cloud classification algorithm and a data-driven 3D model reconstruction method. The Glasgow 3D building model datasets include building footprints with height attributes and 3D models in the level of detail 1 (LoD1) and LoD2. The cross-reference results show that our building footprint aligned well with UK Ordnance Survey data (intersection over union of 82.67% for overlay, R = 0.93 and RMSE = 1.84 m for building height). Building models well represent outer shell features with an average RMSE = 0.54 m for the distance between point clouds and reconstructed models. This accurate 3D building model data can be used in multiple environmental applications for Glasgow, and the open-source data generation workflow can be extended to other major cities for similar applications.

AGORA cities: The hidden infrastructure shaping urban fragility

Eleonora Andreotti; Munazza Usmani; Alice Barsanti; Maurizio Napolitano

卷期
V0 · I0
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1177/23998083261509701
证据层级
OFFICIAL_ONLINE_FIRST

官方论文页面journals.sagepub.com · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

AGORA cities: The hidden infrastructure shaping urban fragility

期刊：Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science · DOI：10.1177/23998083261509701 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Urban services are unevenly distributed across the city, yet it is unclear when their joint presence signals fragility and when it instead outlines a neighbourhood’s vocation. For Bologna and Florence, we integrate Foursquare and OpenStreetMap points of interest with Sentinel-2-derived green indicators based on the 3–30–300 rule, cluster urban areas accordingly, and benchmark the clusters against the demographic, social, economic, and composite fragility indices produced by the municipal statistical offices, which are exogenous to the analysis. In both cities, we identify a subset of infrastructures, termed AGORA (Arts, Green environments, and Open Routes for Access and mobility), whose joint provision is associated with lower fragility. The AGORA Alignment score (AA), based on the rank correlation between AGORA endowment and composite fragility, indicates full alignment in Florence (AA = 1.00) and strong but incomplete alignment in Bologna (AA = 0.80). The remaining service categories instead show wide and heterogeneous distributions: they differentiate areas without tracking fragility and can be interpreted as expressions of urban vocations. The least fragile cluster, the only one with full AGORA endowment, hosts just 3.3% of residents in Bologna and 6.3% in Florence. The framework offers a procedure transferable to other contexts and identifies the expansion of AGORA infrastructures as a candidate lever for reducing fragility without altering existing vocations.

官方论文页面journals.sagepub.com · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

When does post-demolition vacancy become persistent, and how much vacant land remains? Nationwide evidence from South Korea

期刊：Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science · DOI：10.1177/23998083261507758 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Vacant-land inventories show where parcels remain vacant, but not how long they have remained without built reuse after demolition. Using nationwide administrative histories of 435,702 demolished parcels in South Korea (2005–2024), we link demolition dates to subsequent occupancy approvals and estimate Kaplan–Meier still-vacant curves. We identify when these curves enter a sustained period of slow decline, yielding two measures: stabilization time (ST), when the slowdown begins, and stabilization survival (SS), the share remaining without registered built reuse at that point. Under the main three-year persistence criterion, the pooled national curve reaches this phase 1055 days after demolition, with an estimated still-vacant share of 56.4%. Among parcels without observed registered built reuse at the end of 2024, 29% had elapsed durations shorter than this benchmark. Across 1148 neighborhoods, similar ST values can correspond to different SS values, and vice versa. Neighborhood and municipal conditions are more systematically associated with SS than with ST. These findings show that the timing of slowing reuse and the amount of vacancy remaining capture different aspects of post-demolition change.

Predicting economies of scale in Brazilian municipal expenditure: Panel econometrics and interpretable machine learning

Khaled Toffaha; Luan V. Bernardelli; Michael A. Kortt; Albert Wijeweera; Mecit Can Emre Simsekler

卷期
V0 · I0
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1177/23998083261502609
证据层级
OFFICIAL_ONLINE_FIRST

官方论文页面journals.sagepub.com · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Predicting economies of scale in Brazilian municipal expenditure: Panel econometrics and interpretable machine learning

期刊：Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science · DOI：10.1177/23998083261502609 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Municipal size is a persistent question in urban planning and public finance because small jurisdictions may face high per capita costs, whereas larger jurisdictions may face congestion and administrative complexity. This study compares panel econometric and interpretable machine-learning models for real municipal operational expenditure per capita in Brazil. The analysis uses 49,228 municipality-year observations for 5,568 municipalities from 2013 to 2021 and evaluates prediction through municipality-holdout, temporal-holdout, and state-block designs. Histogram Gradient Boosting performs best for unseen municipalities and held-out states, while a municipality fixed-effects-style predictor performs best for later-year prediction of municipalities already observed in the panel. The results indicate strong economies of scale among small municipalities followed by a broad low-cost range rather than one universal optimum. Controlled econometric and spatial-error models imply minima near 143,000 to 176,000 residents, while a reduced-form pooled OLS model excluding grants implies about 232,000 residents. The strongest boosting models show no clear interior upturn within the evaluated grid, with predicted expenditure remaining within 10% of the minimum above approximately 48,000 to 53,000 residents. Grouped SHAP results rank population first, followed by grants and local economic capacity. Residual Moran tests show that unexplained expenditure differences remain spatially clustered. The findings support spatially informed expenditure benchmarking and caution against consolidation policies based on one fixed population threshold.

Quantifying uncertainty in deep learning models for urban safety perception: An MC Dropout approach

Vicente Larraín; Billy Peralta; Hans Lobel

卷期
V0 · I0
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1177/23998083261504070
证据层级
OFFICIAL_ONLINE_FIRST

官方论文页面journals.sagepub.com · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Quantifying uncertainty in deep learning models for urban safety perception: An MC Dropout approach

期刊：Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science · DOI：10.1177/23998083261504070 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Deep learning models have revolutionized the automated analysis of urban perception. However, these models typically provide point predictions without associated uncertainty estimates, limiting their application in critical urban planning and policy decisions. This research explores the implementation of Monte Carlo Dropout (MC Dropout) as a method for quantifying uncertainty in deep learning models for urban safety perception. Using ResNet-based convolutional neural networks, we evaluated this approach on both global urban datasets (PlacePulse 2.0) and a specific case study of Santiago, Chile, focusing specifically on the safety dimension of urban perception. Our findings reveal that MC Dropout yields per-image uncertainty estimates without requiring significant architectural modifications, alongside a modest improvement in predictive accuracy (from 66.81% to 67.64%). We show that these estimates vary systematically with urban characteristics, with distinct patterns observed between different urban districts, and that the MC Dropout spread is systematically narrower than the discrepancy between the stochastic and deterministic predictions. We are explicit about what the study does not establish: none of our analyses tests the uncertainty estimates against predictive error, so we do not claim that they are calibrated or that they rank predictions by reliability, and we specify in detail the risk–coverage protocol and pairwise proper scoring rule that such a validation would require. The potential implications for urban analytics—triage of cases for human verification, mapping the spatial distribution of predictive dispersion within cities, and improved interpretability of model decision-making—are presented as prospective applications conditional on that validation. The methodological framework established here can be adopted and extended for future studies and practical applications in urban environments.

Junhyeong Shim; Bumjoon Kang

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Urban form typologies in Seoul since 1960: A framework for block-level classification and their changing prevalence over time

期刊：Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science · DOI：10.1177/23998083261502967 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

This study proposes a framework for classifying urban form typologies at the block level and examining changes in their prevalence in Seoul since 1960. The study identifies major typologies aligned with urban planning trends and analyzes their morphological characteristics. Key questions addressed include: how many distinct urban form types can be identified in Seoul; what unique features define each type; and how changes in the prevalence of these types over time reflect broader urban development trends. The analytical procedure consisted of three steps. First, block units were defined based on infrastructure boundaries such as arterial roads. Second, eight indicators were established to capture the morphological features of blocks, parcels, streets, and buildings—the fundamental components of urban structure. Third, Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM) clustering was applied to classify blocks with similar numerical indicators into five distinct types. The result effectively captures how many morphological features aligned with Seoul’s urban planning objectives by era. Compared with previous typologies, the study integrates multiple scales of urban form and examines changes in typology prevalence across different periods, providing a basis for interpreting the historical process of urbanization.

What defines the linear city? Examining accessibility premises with novel flow-based linearity indices

Yongki Kim

卷期
V0 · I0
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1177/23998083261500494
证据层级
OFFICIAL_ONLINE_FIRST

官方论文页面journals.sagepub.com · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

What defines the linear city? Examining accessibility premises with novel flow-based linearity indices

期刊：Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science · DOI：10.1177/23998083261500494 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

This study re-examines the widely held assumption that the linear city model enhances both efficiency and equity of accessibility. From a space syntax perspective, we introduce two flow-based linearity indices: the Morphological Linearity Index, which captures how strongly path-implied trips align along a single axis, and the Structural Linearity Index, which captures how strongly paths converge on shared street segments. We then relate these indices to accessibility measures that are likewise derived from network configuration alone. Applying these indices to 60 South Korean cities, we find that corridor-centered structures alone do not guarantee efficient or equitable accessibility. Networks with higher morphological linearity show lower mean detour rates yet no corresponding reduction in average trip distance, a directness-distance trade-off that can offset net efficiency gains. Higher structural linearity is associated with both greater detour rates and longer trip distances, suggesting that corridor-centered designs can undermine overall accessibility. By translating the “linear city” into measurable indices, this study provides an operational basis for evidence-based road network planning and assessment.

Generative AI for urban planning: Designing streetscapes from street-view imagery using diffusion models

Yuzhou Chen; Yuebing Liang; Lingqian Hu; Kailai Sun; Qingqi Song; Chang Zhao; Shenhao Wang

10.1177/23998083261500883

官方论文页面journals.sagepub.com · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Generative AI for urban planning: Designing streetscapes from street-view imagery using diffusion models

期刊：Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science · DOI：10.1177/23998083261500883 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Street-view imagery (SVI) is widely used to quantify key indicators of urban environment, such as greenery, sky, or road view indices. However, relatively few studies have examined controlled SVI synthesis that combines explicit target proportions of trees, sky, buildings, and roads with road-layout masks, while also testing conflicts between textual and spatial controls. To address this gap, we propose a generative multimodal AI framework that synthesizes alternative streetscapes conditioned on targeted visual metrics, enabling direct visual exploration of urban scenarios. We first construct a multimodal dataset that aligns SVIs with textual descriptions, segmentation maps, road masks, and quantitative metrics of visual elements in Chicago and Orlando. Using this dataset, we demonstrate that diffusion models can produce realistic and semantically consistent streetscape imagery while responding to both textual and imagery controls. Our quantitative evaluations show that incorporating visual controls can improve semantic consistency, reducing the LPIPS index by approximately 6% while maintaining global visual realism. In addition, overall semantic consistency increases by 23.7% in Orlando and 46.4% in Chicago, as measured by the mIoU index, with class-wise gains exceeding 100% improvement for building view indices. Streetscape generation shows directional and interpretable responses to both visual and textual prompts, and when textual and visual controls conflict, imagery controls tend to dominate, indicating a clear control hierarchy and the importance of further developing visual controls for urban scene generation. Overall, this work establishes an important benchmark for streetscape generation using SVIs and diffusion models, and illustrates how generative AI can support controllable and exploratory urban scenario visualization.

Assessing the association between street view–derived perceived liveliness and street thefts

Hanlin Zhou; Lin Liu; Minxuan Lan; Devin Yongzhao Wu; Zhang Chen; Xin Gu; Zihan Su

卷期
V0 · I0
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1177/23998083261502943
证据层级
OFFICIAL_ONLINE_FIRST

官方论文页面journals.sagepub.com · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Assessing the association between street view–derived perceived liveliness and street thefts

期刊：Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science · DOI：10.1177/23998083261502943 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Crime geographers are attentive to how the spatial characteristics of the environment affect criminal behaviors. Recently, scholars have found that aside from physical and social attributes, the perception of the environment, such as safety perception, is also associated with crime occurrence. However, perceived liveliness, a visual perceptual proxy for potential routine activity settings that are closely linked to routine activity theory and crime pattern theory, is rarely explored in prior research. This study examines the association between perceived liveliness and street thefts in the City of Cincinnati, the United States. The liveliness perception scores are derived from street view imagery using a support vector machine model trained on the MIT Place Pulse V2 crowdsourcing image survey. Negative binomial regression models are used to test the relationships between perceived liveliness and street theft, with social and physical environmental attributes controlled. Results show that perceived liveliness significantly improves model performance and is positively associated with street theft. This study provides the first evidence that perceived liveliness helps explain crimes. The positive relationship between perceived liveliness of the built environment and crime adds a new dimension to the routine activity theory and the crime pattern theory. It also offers a scalable and cost-effective measure that can assist urban planners and law enforcement in identifying potential theft hotspots using readily available street view imagery.

Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science · ONLINE_FIRST

A national dataset of downtown business district boundaries for pre-automobile U.S. cities and towns

Andrew J. Van Leuven

卷期
V0 · I0
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1177/23998083261500874
证据层级
OFFICIAL_ONLINE_FIRST

官方论文页面journals.sagepub.com · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

A national dataset of downtown business district boundaries for pre-automobile U.S. cities and towns

期刊：Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science · DOI：10.1177/23998083261500874 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

The downtown business district is a fundamental unit of analysis in urban revitalization research, yet no nationally consistent, publicly available dataset of downtown boundaries exists for small and mid-sized U.S. cities and towns. This paper introduces an open data product containing 6,551 downtown polygons for pre-automobile-era places spanning 47 contiguous states. The dataset is constructed from a fully reproducible, open-source R workflow: a national universe of historically significant places is assembled using historical census records, contemporary population data, and metropolitan classification criteria; points-of-interest data from the Overture Maps Foundation are collected within each place boundary; and a kernel density estimation algorithm identifies the highest-concentration commercial cluster as the downtown core. The resulting boundaries are validated against Main Street program districts, which the polygons cover at high rates in the two states examined. The polygons are deposited at Harvard Dataverse under a CC0 1.0 license, with all R code available on GitHub. The dataset supports research on downtown revitalization, Main Street commercial activity, and the economic geography of small and mid-sized American cities.

Exploratory geoplay in metaverse fieldwork: Avatar movement as learning data for geographic understanding

Kotaro Iizuka; Hiroyuki Yamauchi; Takuro Ogura

卷期
V0 · I0
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1177/23998083261501338
证据层级
OFFICIAL_ONLINE_FIRST

官方论文页面journals.sagepub.com · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Exploratory geoplay in metaverse fieldwork: Avatar movement as learning data for geographic understanding

期刊：Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science · DOI：10.1177/23998083261501338 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Geogames are increasingly recognized as playful systems based on real-world geographic data and spatial reasoning, yet open-ended geographic play remains underexplored. This study examines immersive virtual fieldwork as exploratory geoplay, where participants engage with geographic environments through autonomous observation, movement, and interaction rather than scores, competition, or fixed missions. We developed a multiplayer metaverse environment based on a life-scale 3D reconstruction of the Byobugaura Sea Cliff in Japan. Sixty-two participants explored it in two rounds: one unguided and one supported by light interpretive prompts. Avatar positions were recorded using the YAIBA behavior-logging system and analyzed through space-time clustering, PCA, trajectory-grid aggregation, and cluster-duration analysis. During unguided exploration, participants clustered around geomorphologically significant areas, while interpretive supports shifted visits toward breakwater and terrace zones. Cluster durations increased in the smaller sub-instance but remained stable in the larger main instance. Questionnaire responses reflected positive experiences and perceived behavioral changes. By framing avatar movement as learning data, while recognizing that movement does not directly measure knowledge acquisition, this study proposes exploratory geoplay as a model for studying locational attention and geographic inquiry within immersive environments.

Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science · ONLINE_FIRST

Optimising resource management in data-scarce environments: A modelling approach for Greater Cairo

Eman Abou Ziyan; Richard Kingston; Joe Ravetz

卷期
V0 · I0
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1177/23998083261500938
证据层级
OFFICIAL_ONLINE_FIRST

官方论文页面journals.sagepub.com · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Optimising resource management in data-scarce environments: A modelling approach for Greater Cairo

期刊：Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science · DOI：10.1177/23998083261500938 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Pressing environmental challenges present significant obstacles to cities’ sustainable development and underscore the need for effective resource management. However, resource management studies often overlook or oversimplify the spatial and social dimensions of resource use, limiting their ability to target interventions to specific population groups and integrate them into urban planning. Addressing this requires disaggregated data that capture spatial variation and interdependencies between socio-economic factors and resource consumption. Yet such data are often scarce in Global South contexts, where data limitations frequently hinder meaningful resource management research. This research develops a hybrid multi-scale analysis framework that integrates Spatial Microsimulation, Multi-Scale Geographically Weighted Regression (MGWR), and Geodemographic Classification to help researchers address data limitations and generate policy-relevant insights. The framework offers three contributions: methodological, empirical, and policy translation. The research provides the first systematic, household-level analysis of food-water-energy consumption patterns across Greater Cairo. Spatial microsimulation creates robust synthetic household datasets by integrating multiple surveys, while MGWR reveals spatially varying drivers of consumption. The geodemographic classification delivers the first empirically grounded typology of Greater Cairo households based on demographic, socio-economic, and consumption characteristics. Lastly, the applied probability-based allocation of clusters to neighbourhoods offers an indicative tool for exploring differentiated tariff structures and area-specific demand management, validated through expert workshops. By addressing Greater Cairo’s data limitations, the framework offers a replicable approach for other cities facing similar constraints, supporting the transition to evidence-based, spatially differentiated resource management in the Global South.

Carbon emission and health impacts of remote working: A case study of the Helsinki capital region

Alonso Espinosa Mireles de Villafranca; Petr Anashin; Bhagya Nagakamkanamge

卷期
V0 · I0
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1177/23998083261500928
证据层级
OFFICIAL_ONLINE_FIRST

官方论文页面journals.sagepub.com · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-20 12:12 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Carbon emission and health impacts of remote working: A case study of the Helsinki capital region

期刊：Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science · DOI：10.1177/23998083261500928 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Remote working can reduce commuting-related carbon emissions but may also lower population physical activity levels. This study examines these trade-offs in the Helsinki Capital Region by modelling changes in commuting and worktime travel under three scenarios: no remote work, current levels, and full potential uptake. We combine demographic data, the Finnish National Travel Survey, public transport schedules, and official transport demand models to estimate impacts on CO 2 emissions and active travel. Commuting volumes are taken from demographic data containing home and workplace information of workers, and worktime trips are modelled using an agent-based synthetic population model. Under the full remote work scenario, commuting-related emissions decrease by approximately 72.9 tonnes per day, a 16.7% reduction compared to current levels. However, rebound trips during working hours offset 71.3% of these potential savings, even after accounting for reduced workplace-based trips. Health impacts were assessed using the WHO Health Economic Assessment Tool. Compared to a baseline with no remote work, current remote working levels are associated with approximately 42 additional premature deaths annually due to reduced physical activity, with a further 31 deaths under full remote work conditions. Conversely, if commuting time were repurposed for walking or cycling, up to 71 premature deaths could be avoided under current conditions and 137 under full remote working. The findings highlight the interrelationship between environmental and health dimensions of remote work. While remote working involves clear trade-offs, integrated policy approaches that combine emission reduction strategies and promotion of physical activity while limiting rebound travel are needed to achieve substantial co-benefits.

Helium Mass Spectrometer Leak Detector

作者未在官方目录完整给出

卷期
V41 · IZ1

主题与阅读路线

以下聚类仅依据题名与论文类型等元数据，不从题目推断方法或结果。

  • Environment and Planning B 2026-V53-I6 题名多见步行网络、邻里边界、迁居与住房、城市形态与空间可视化等，另有 3 篇社论；I7 题名多见热环境、快速公交与住房、城市蔓延、自行车路径与小区域普查数据等；I8 题名多见绿地可达性、夜间灯光、数字经济与城市扩张、微出行与 GeoAI 等；I9（当期）题名多见步行舒适、洪泛区产权、城市活力、公交可达与住房冲突等。官方 OnlineFirst 新条目另有城市脆弱性、地理游戏等题名，尚未归入正式期次。
  • 中国科学院院刊新登记发现条目中，Crossref 标注 I9/I10 的题名需待官方目录恢复后才能作为正式期次阅读；Z1 预出版条目题名多见科学仪器与装置介绍。官方站点不可达，不进入正式期次阅读路线。

数据源健康

  • REG-020 Environment and Planning B: Urban Analytics and City Science：OFFICIAL_PAGE_OK_VIA_PLAYWRIGHT — SAGE 官方目录 curl 均为 Cloudflare 403（Just a moment / cf-mitigated: challenge），但 Playwright 成功打开官方当期、过刊目录与 OnlineFirst。官方 All Issues / 2026 年组列出 Volume 53 Number 1–9，当期标题为 Volume 53, Number 9。gate-issue 对 I6、I7、I8、I9 均返回 NEW_ISSUE；官方 TOC 稳定且页码连续，予以 claim：2026-V53-I6（17 篇，页码 1187–1418）、2026-V53-I7（15 篇，1421–1666）、2026-V53-I8（14 篇，1669–1912）、2026-V53-I9（当期，15 篇，1915–2156）。61 篇正式论文中归期 48 篇（原 Online First 按 DOI 合并），新记录 13 篇。研究论文摘要优先取 Crossref 全文；社论/公告等无摘要条目标为暂无摘要，不编造。注册基线 2026-V53-I5 仍列于官方过刊目录，不等于新完整期，不 claim、不重印。官方 OnlineFirst 共 11 页 101 条，对照账本新登记 12 条，已知抑制 89 条。
  • REG-050 中国科学院院刊：OFFICIAL_UNREACHABLE_CAS — 中国科学院院刊官方站点本轮不可达：HTTPS 出现 TLS unexpected EOF，HTTP 返回 500（Pingora），Playwright 同样失败（ERR_CONNECTION_CLOSED / HTTP 500）。不得把 Crossref 当作完整期证据，不 claim。已见表完整期仍为 2026-V41-I8，retry_flag 保持 true。Crossref ISSN 1000-3045 仅作发现：在排除已见表/基线 I1–I8 后，新登记 142 条（标注 V41-I9 17、V41-I10 16、V41-IZ1 预出版/增刊 106、无卷期 3），同范围已知抑制 47 条。Crossref 未提供这些条目的摘要，标为暂无摘要，不编造。候选期次按发现源更新为 2026-V41-I10，待官方目录恢复后再做 issue gate。
  • REG-080 Applied Soft Computing：OFFICIAL_403_SCIENCEDIRECT — Applied Soft Computing 官方 ScienceDirect 首页/卷期列表/Articles in Press/V197 与候选卷目录，curl 均为 HTTP 403（Cloudflare cf-mitigated: challenge），Playwright 停留在 “Just a moment...” 挑战页，未能确认后续卷是否已结束 In progress、目录是否稳定。已见表完整期仍为 2026-V197，不得根据 RSS 或 Crossref claim V198+，本刊结果记为 DEGRADED，retry_flag 保持 true。ScienceDirect RSS curl 200（100 条）仅作发现：全部为账本中尚未登记的条目，新登记 100 条（RSS 描述 Volume 205 68、Volume 206 14、未标卷号 18），已知抑制 0 条。Crossref 未提供这些条目的摘要，标为暂无摘要，不编造。RSS 描述 Volume 206 出版日期为 February 2027，属滚动出版中的未来/在编容器，即使条目数看起来完整也不得 claim。候选期次按发现源更新为 2026-V206。
